022378:     function addOrUpdateVehicleRow(originalName, mappedName, required, status, selected) {
022379:         const safeRequired = Math.max(0, parseInt(required, 10) || 0);

007514:     function personnelMatchesRule(person, rule) {

007516:         if (!requiredCodes.length) return true;

007522:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
007523:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
007524:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007525:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007526:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
007527: 
007528:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
007529:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
007530:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
007531:         });
007532:     }

007514:     function personnelMatchesRule(person, rule) {
007515:         const requiredCodes = Array.isArray(rule?.trainingAll) ? rule.trainingAll : [];
007516:         if (!requiredCodes.length) return true;
007517: 
007518:         const trainingSet = getPersonnelTrainingSet(person);
007519:         return requiredCodes.every(code => trainingSet.has(code));
007520:     }

007522:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
007523:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
007524:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007525:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007526:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
007527: 
007528:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
007529:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
007530:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
007531:         });
007532:     }

007554:             .find(value => /^\d+$/.test(value));
007555:

007563:             row.querySelector?.('td:nth-child(2)')?.textContent
007564:         ].filter(Boolean).join(' '));
007565: 
007566:         // MissionChief UK PSU Carrier. This fallback is used only when the
007567:         // station row exposes no numeric vehicle-type attribute at all.
007568:         if (/\b(?:PSU|Police\s+Support\s+Unit)\s+Carrier\b/i.test(typeText)) {
007569:             return '51';
007570:         }
007571: 
007572:         return '';
007573:     }

007575:     function getPersonnelVehicleQueue(doc, allowedTypeIds) {
007576:         const allowed = new Set((allowedTypeIds || []).map(String));
007577:         const allowAllVehicleTypes = allowed.size === 0;
007578:         const seen = new Set();
007579:         const queue = [];
007580: 
007581:         for (const row of [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')]) {
007582:             const vehicleTypeId = getPersonnelVehicleTypeIdFromRow(row);
007583:             if (!allowAllVehicleTypes && !allowed.has(vehicleTypeId)) continue;
007584: 
007585:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
007586:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
007587:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
007588:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
007589:             const vehicleId = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
007590:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
007591:                 .find(Boolean) || '';
007592: 
007593:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
007594:             seen.add(vehicleId);
007595: 
007596:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
007597:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''));
007598:             queue.push({
007599:                 vehicleId,
007600:                 vehicleTypeId,
007601:                 name: cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `${TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[vehicleTypeId] || 'Vehicle'} ${vehicleId}`,
007602:                 assignmentHref: directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`,
007603:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
007604:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
007605:             });
007606:         }
007607: 
007608:         queue.sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name, 'en-GB', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' }));
007609:         return queue;
007610:     }

008893:     function getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue(doc) {
008894:         const seen = new Set();
008895:         const queue = [];
008896:         const rows = [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')];
008897:         let ambulanceRows = 0;
008898:

008900:             const image = row.querySelector('img[vehicle_type_id]');

008902:             if (vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) continue;
008903: 
008904:             ambulanceRows++;
008905: 
008906:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];

008908:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
008909:                 return /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(href);
008910:             });
008911:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
008912:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
008913:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
008914:             });
008915:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
008916:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
008917:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
008918:             });
008919: 
008920:             // Prefer the normal vehicle-name link because it is tied directly to the
008921:             // displayed row. Edit and personnel links are safe fallbacks.
008922:             const idCandidates = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
008923:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
008924:                 .filter(Boolean);
008925:             const vehicleId = idCandidates[0] || '';
008926: 
008927:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
008928:             seen.add(vehicleId);
008929: 
008930:             const distinctIds = [...new Set(idCandidates)];
008931:             if (distinctIds.length > 1) {
008932:                 personnelDebug(`Ambulance row contains conflicting vehicle IDs (${distinctIds.join(', ')}). Using ${vehicleId}.`);
008933:             }
008934: 
008935:             const assignmentHref = directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`;
008936:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink
008937:                 || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''))
008938:                 || null;
008939:             const name = cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `Ambulance ${vehicleId}`;
008940:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
008941: 
008942:             queue.push({
008943:                 vehicleId,
008944:                 name,
008945:                 assignmentHref,
008946:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
008947:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
008948:             });
008949:         }
008950: 
008951:         queue.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, {
008952:             numeric: true,
008953:             sensitivity: 'base'
008954:         }));
008955: 
008956:         personnelDebug(`Vehicle table scan: ${rows.length} row(s), ${ambulanceRows} Ambulance row(s), ${queue.length} usable assignment route(s).`);
008957:         queue.forEach(vehicle => {
008958:             personnelDebug(`Ambulance detected: ${vehicle.name} | ID=${vehicle.vehicleId} | table occupancy=${vehicle.assignedCount ?? '?'} / ${vehicle.maxCount ?? '?'}`);
008959:         });
008960: 
008961:         return queue;
008962:     }

011302:     function isMissionFinderIosSafariWebsite() {
011303:         const userAgent = String(navigator.userAgent || '');
011304:         const platform = String(navigator.platform || '');
011305:         const isIosDevice = /iP(?:ad|hone|od)/i.test(userAgent)
011306:             || (
011307:                 platform === 'MacIntel'
011308:                 && Number(navigator.maxTouchPoints || 0) > 1
011309:             );
011310:         const isSafariBrowser = /Safari/i.test(userAgent)
011311:             && !/(?:CriOS|FxiOS|EdgiOS|OPiOS|DuckDuckGo)/i.test(userAgent);
011312: 
011313:         return isIosDevice
011314:             && isSafariBrowser
011315:             && /^https?:$/i.test(String(location.protocol || ''));
011316:     }

011318:     function getMissionFinderPhoneScreenShortSide() {
011319:         const values = [];
011320: 
011321:         const addValue = value => {
011322:             const number = Number(value);
011323: 
011324:             if (
011325:                 Number.isFinite(number) &&
011326:                 number > 0
011327:             ) {
011328:                 values.push(number);
011329:             }
011330:         };
011331: 
011332:         try {
011333:             addValue(window.screen?.width);
011334:             addValue(window.screen?.height);
011335:         } catch (_error) {}
011336: 
011337:         if (values.length >= 2) {
011338:             return Math.min(...values);
011339:         }
011340: 
011341:         try {
011342:             addValue(window.visualViewport?.width);
011343:             addValue(window.innerWidth);
011344:             addValue(document.documentElement?.clientWidth);
011345:         } catch (_error) {}
011346: 
011347:         return values.length
011348:             ? Math.min(...values)
011349:             : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
011350:     }

003822:     function getPersonnelUnitsRemaining() {
003823:         return isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()
003824:             ? PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired
003825:             : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

003827: 
003828: 
003829:     function formatPersonnelUnitsFulfilled() {
003830:         if (isPersonnelPerStationTargetMode()) {
003831:             const target = PERSONNEL_STATE.unitsRequired;
003832:             const latestFulfilled = PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport
003833:                 && !PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport.error
003834:                     ? Math.min(
003835:                         target,
003836:                         Number(
003837:                             PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport.fulfilledUnits
003838:                             || 0
003839:                         )
003840:                     )
003841:                     : 0;
003842: 
003843:             return `${PERSONNEL_STATE.stationsTargetMet} / ${PERSONNEL_STATE.completedStations} stations met | latest ${latestFulfilled}/${target}`;
003844:         }

004450:     function getStationNamingTypeInfo(buildingTypeId, displayName) {
004451:         const name = cleanText(displayName).toUpperCase();
004452: 
004453:         // Airport fire stations share MissionChief's normal fire-station type IDs.
004454:         // Use an explicit existing name marker only for this special case.
004455:         if ((buildingTypeId === 0 || buildingTypeId === 18)
004456:             && (/\bAIRPORT\b|\bAIRFIELD\b/.test(name) || /-AF\d*$/.test(name))) {
004457:             return { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' };
004458:         }
004459: 
004460:         const mapped = STATION_BUILDING_TYPE_INFO[buildingTypeId];
004461:         if (mapped) return mapped;
004462: 
004463:         const detectedType = detectStationType(displayName);
004464:         const fallbackByType = {
004465:             FIRE: { stationType: 'FIRE', suffix: '-FS', label: 'Fire station' },
004466:             AMBULANCE: { stationType: 'AMBULANCE', suffix: '-AS', label: 'Ambulance station' },
004467:             POLICE: { stationType: 'POLICE', suffix: '-PS', label: 'Police station' },
004468:             RNLI: { stationType: 'RNLI', suffix: '-RNLI', label: 'RNLI / lifeboat station' },
004469:             COASTGUARD: { stationType: 'COASTGUARD', suffix: '-CG', label: 'Coastguard station' },
004470:             SAR: { stationType: 'SAR', suffix: '-SAR', label: 'Search and rescue station' },
004471:             AIRFIELD: { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' },
004472:             EOD: { stationType: 'EOD', suffix: '-EOD', label: 'EOD / bomb disposal station' },
004473:             RECOVERY: { stationType: 'RECOVERY', suffix: '-REC', label: 'Recovery station' }
004474:         };
004475: 
004476:         if (detectedType === 'AIR') {
004477:             return /POLICE/i.test(name)
004478:                 ? { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-PH', label: 'Police helicopter station' }
004479:                 : { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-AA', label: 'Air ambulance station' };
004480:         }
004481: 
004482:         return fallbackByType[detectedType]
004483:             || { stationType: 'OTHER', suffix: '', label: `Unsupported building type ${buildingTypeId ?? 'unknown'}` };
004484:     }

009230:     async function submitPersonnelAssignment(candidate) {
009231:         if (!candidate.assignHref || PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return false;
009232: 
009233:         const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.getAttribute('content') || '';
009234:         const headers = {
009235:             'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml',
009236:             'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
009237:         };
009238:         if (csrfToken) headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = csrfToken;
009239: 
009240:         const response = await personnelFetchResponse(candidate.assignHref, {
009241:             method: 'POST',
009242:             headers
009243:         }, 12000);
009244: 
009245:         if (!response.ok) {
009246:             personnelDebug(`Assignment request HTTP ${response.status}: ${candidate.assignHref}`);
009247:             return false;
009248:         }
009249: 
009250:         // Consume the response so the request finishes cleanly. MissionChief normally
009251:         // returns the updated personnel-row HTML, but the batch is verified using one
009252:         // fresh vehicle page after all required people have been submitted.
009253:         try {
009254:             await response.text();
009255:         } catch (_error) {}
009256: 
009257:         return true;
009258:     }
